## 3. Open questions

Rows are kept in the order they were opened. A row is *Answered* when a finding settles it, *Closed* when the project's direction made it moot (D18 in particular), and open rows carry their plan as of their latest dated update.

| # | Question | Plan |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Q1 | *Answered 2026-09-30 → F23; closed 2026-10-08.* Yes: userptr BO mapped at GPU VA == CPU VA, zero copies; `munmap` under a mapping → F26. The coherence sub-question is answered in practice: guest memory is mapped as dma-bufs at the guest's addresses (F40, F55) and the game runs hours of play and 1.3 million submissions without a coherence fault (F59, F85). | — |
| Q2 | *Answered 2026-09-29 → F12; slots answered 2026-09-30 → F19:* `contextControl` is a CONTEXT_CONTROL packet, `cmds[0]` the state preamble IB, `cmds[1]` a 2-dword IB (NOP), `cmds[2]` unused in the first submit; the frame DCB comes through `0xc0188132`. | — |
| Q3 | *Answered 2026-09-30 → F21.* Yes: every compute dispatch preamble writes `COMPUTE_STATIC_THREAD_MGMT_SE0..3 = 0xffffffff`; graphics buffers write none. BC5's mask is ANDed into those writes. | — |
| Q4 | Which draw/CB/DB register usage is Sony-specific beyond public PM4? *First data 2026-09-30 → F21:* one unresolved SH offset (mm 0x2e80) and one APU-only opcode (0x8e). | *Parked 2026-10-08:* nothing left unresolved blocks ASTRO BOT (F85); reopens with the next title, whose captures extend `docs/formats/agc.md`. *Reopened 2026-10-09 by the second title:* compute-queue `DISPATCH_INDIRECT` with an address instead of an offset (F90, F91; public AMD form, new to this host), and a `LOAD_SH_REG_INDEX` that loads zero dwords, which the host's operand check takes for a null address (F92, F95; no-op, dropping it is harmless). *2026-10-19:* the SH offset 0x2e80 is the driver's "write nowhere" register (F111, F112; AnyPS5's name for it), and opcode 0x8e has not recurred. Open after the second title reached gameplay: the constant uconfig write 0xc383 (F208; forcing it to 0 changes nothing), the title's own `SET_BASE` [1,0,0] with small offsets ahead of compute-form `DISPATCH_INDIRECT`s (F209, TODO(verify) what the console's CP reads there), and the HS offchip value of `/dev/gc` ioctl 0x8004812a (0x1ff, F248, TODO(verify)); the geometry-engine base was the TF ring the title hands to `/dev/gc` (F248, Q13 answered). Plan: fold the three into `docs/formats/agc.md` as the independently written AGC driver of Q14 is specified from the public packet set; each one is settled by a run that reproduces the console's value, never by assumption. |
| Q5 | Storage: how much of the SSD/Kraken dependency can a software prefetch/cache hide? *Partly answered 2026-10-06 → F51, F71:* the level load was the host's own lookups, not storage (240 s → 135 s → 25 s); the board's M.2 is idle at 16 MiB/s of 700+. A title that leans on the hardware decompressor is a new question when one is tried. |

Remaining: the graphics stages' 16-bit `CU_EN` — which of an engine's 20 CUs it can select. | *Parked 2026-10-08:* the game runs on all 40 CUs with the masks it writes; which CUs `CU_EN` selects matters only for CU partitioning, not for playing. *2026-10-10 → F97:* in the game, `CU_EN` bits 0–9 carry work and bits 10–15 none (0x03ff = 0xffff, 0x00ff 15 % slower), consistent with one bit per CU of a 10-CU shader array, TODO(verify) with a pixel-shader-bound probe. *Closed 2026-10-19:* F97 is the answer for play — bits 0–9 carry work, one per CU of a 10-CU shader array (TODO(verify) with a pixel-shader-bound probe) — and D5's 36/4 partition is in no play setting: both titles run on all 40 CUs (F97; 36 CUs changed nothing for the hang of F184 either), so which CUs `CU_EN` selects matters only if partitioning returns. |

Submission chains per ioctl (ADR 0010, F213) cut the kernel submissions by 39 % but not the render thread's frame; with `COND_EXEC` segment-local and the game thread's chain held across its ioctls (ADR 0010 §A/§B, `BC5_DIRECT_CHAIN=2`) the night scene runs at 45 flips/s instead of 33, 1,658 instead of 4,463 kernel submissions a second, the render thread 93 → 83 % (F218, experiment 0165, verdict pending). | Steps (1) and (2) of experiment 0163's verdict are done (F218). ## 4. Architecture (summary)

```
PS5 game (eboot.bin, PRX)  →  KytyPlus host: loader, libkernel and every system library written independently (HLE; D18, ADR 0012)
                               ├ today: the game's own libSceAgc/libSceAgcDriver, LLE-loaded from a game dump's fakelib/ (D11; the pack, Q14)
                               └ next:  an independently written AGC driver in the host (Q14)
                            →  /dev/gc boundary  →  BC5 backend (direct path, ADR 0005/0006)  →  amdgpu/libdrm  →  BC-250
                                                     ├→ I/O layer (bc5-mount: .ffpfsc and .pkg, parallel reads, decode-ahead, disk cache)
                                                     └→ audio + input (host: PipeWire, SDL; ATRAC9 haptic tracks decoded by vendored vgmstream (F301), sent as audio to the DualSense; without its sink, rumble on a worker thread (F329, F330))
```

One host, one track: RPCSX is a reference for the `/dev/gc` layouts and the PM4 synchronisation packets (F12), not a host (D18). The backend's host interface is the `/dev/gc` boundary (F12, F18): submit ioctls, GPU memory mapping (`mmap`, flexible/tool memory), `kevent` completion, EOP flip and buffer labels. Whatever builds the command stream behind that boundary — the LLE pair today, the host's own driver tomorrow — gets the same backend.

Backend modes: (1) validation — decode + log, submit nothing (the captures of experiments 0011–0012); (2) hybrid — never built; (3) direct — own libdrm client, the guest's memory mapped 1:1 as dma-bufs of the host's backing files (ADR 0006), the packets filtered and rewritten for `amdgpu`'s command processor (the context-state stack, the label hand-overs, CP DMA waits: F34–F42), jobs in flight on the GFX ring with the title's asynchronous compute on the BC-250's compute rings (ADR 0009, F177–F181), graphics pieces behind a full-state prefix at cross-queue waits (ADR 0008), a register-state prefix before drawing buffers against state inherited from the desktop (F204), and submission chains (`BC5_DIRECT_CHAIN=2`, in the launchers; ADR 0010, F213, F218, F220), with the held tail on top of them (ADR 0013, F312). Everything that submits is behind `BC5_GC_MODE=direct` and `BC5_DIRECT_STAGE` (hard rule 5).

**The direct path today (2026-10-24).**
- **Where the code is.** The host side is in patch 0001's `bc5LleAgc.cpp`: the `/dev/gc` emulation, ring consumer, chains, held tail, prefixes, timeout probe and Ray tracing switch (F263, F317, F321, F343). The rest is `backend/`, linked as `libbc5_backend.a`: the policy filter `policy.cpp`, the CP-side indirect check `indirect_check.cpp` and the libdrm device `direct.cpp` (F263, F336, Q19).
- **Rings.** The game threads' graphics buffers go to the GFX ring; the `bc5-ring` thread takes the title's asynchronous compute buffers to compute ring 0 (ADR 0009, F178). A graphics wait on a compute-written label is kept for the GPU in ring order (ADR 0013 §Context). The second title runs on two rings; ASTRO BOT's `play-astro.sh` on one, `play-astro-compute.sh` on two (F181, F315, F327).
- **Chains and the held tail.** `BC5_DIRECT_CHAIN=2` is in the launchers (ADR 0010, F218, F220). At a wait no submitted job serves, the title's thread is no longer held: the wait stays for the GPU, the thread's chain is held, and `bc5-hold` submits it once the label is met or its writer is on a ring (ADR 0013, F312; on by default, `BC5_DIRECT_HOLD_TAIL=0` turns it off; it acts only with chains, a compute ring and cross-ring waits).
- **Prefixes and checks.** The full-state prefix goes before drawing buffers (F204); the compute-state prefix before buffers that dispatch on an inherited program (F253, F321; on in `play-pkg.sh`). The CP checks indirect dispatch arguments and skips absurd counts (exp 0052, F106, F107; noted in F336).
- **Second-title settings (D15).** The TF ring (F248), the FLAT-aperture rewrite (F249, F277), the AMM window as PRT (F257, F277), `BC5_DIRECT_SKIP_PGM=909974400` (F277), and `KYTY_BC5_RT_BUILD_PGM`, the ten BVH build programs the Ray tracing switch drops when off (F343).
- **Hang records.** `play-pkg.sh` sets the timeout probe: at a ring timeout it journals a stuck buffer's waits, label writes and dispatches with their grids, and fsyncs them (F317, F334); the lean build compiles it out. The maintainer's udev rule saves each GPU devcoredump (CLAUDE.md, F336).
- **Host side, on by default in both shared builds.** APR workers with read-ahead (F203, F273, F288), the tiler scratch pool (F311), the ATRAC9 vibration decoder (F301), SaveData sidecars (F319, F324), the pad rumble worker with the non-blocking pad speaker (F330).

CU policy: D5's 36/4 split exists as a switch (`BC5_DIRECT_CU_MASK`, ANDed with the masks the titles write, F39/F39a) and is in no play setting; both titles run on all 40 CUs (F97, Q8).

## 5. Phases and gates

See `docs/PHASES.md`. Order as run: 0a `bc5-mount` → 1b KytyPlus + LLE `libSceAgc` (D11) → 1 AGC validation → 2 native shaders → 3 IB submit → 5 play (current). Gates G0a, G1b, G2 and G3 are passed. G1 is not recorded as a gate (its substance was shown in experiments 0011–0012). G5 (a 60-minute session of the second title on the default shared build with no GPU hang, fault or machine-down, with the kernel stream) is proposed for the maintainer to confirm. Phases 0b (RPCSX baseline) and 4 (the backend inside RPCSX) named a host that boots the console's system software and are retired by D18 (ADR 0012). Phase 5's open work is in §3.

## 6. Related projects (what to read, and for what)

| Project | Read for |
| --- | --- |
| Coder787-source/KytyPlus (GPL-2.0) | **The host.** HLE system libraries, loader, kernel, audio, input; our changes are `backend/kytyplus-patches/0001-bc5-lle-agc.patch` against `f266548`; upstream since `f266548` (328 library commits): `docs/ports/upstream-sync-review.md`, two hunks kept in commit e7fbf9b (F335) and built privately in exp 0276; `src/loader/runtimeLinker.cpp` LLE loader (`SHADPS4_SYSMODULES_PACK_DIR`, to be phased out, Q14); `src/kernel/memory.cpp` guest memory sizing |
| KytyPS5/KytyPS5 (GPL-2.0) | the upstream of KytyPlus's HLE libraries: `src/libs/libAmpr.cpp` (per-priority APR engine), `ajm/atrac9_decoder.h`, `libSystemService.cpp` (F76, F89); the trigger-effect state machine (F78); `src/graphics/guest_gpu` (AGC decoding) and `shader/recompiler` as cross-checks. Does not start on the BC-250 (F187) |

Caveat: its debt notes citing an SDK reference are not carried over (review §1) |
| RPCSX/rpcsx (GPL-2.0) | **Reference for the AGC/GPU side only** (D18): `rpcsx/iodev/gc.cpp` for the `/dev/gc` ioctl codes and layouts (F12); `rpcsx/gpu/Pipe.cpp` for `RELEASE_MEM`, `WRITE_DATA`, `WAIT_REG_MEM`, `EVENT_WRITE_EOP`, `INDIRECT_BUFFER` in the BC5 soft CP; `rpcsx/gpu/Device.cpp` for its 2^40-byte per-process GPU window (F92). Not a host: it boots the console's system modules, which this project does not use |

| SvenGDK/LibProsperoPKG (GPL-3) | PS5 package builder/reader in C#: `docs/ps5-pkg-format.md`, naps layout, data-first inner image, Kraken decoder with the excess framing — documentation only (F82) |

## 7. Decisions taken so far

- D1 Project name **BC5** (BC-250 + PS5). Repo `bc5`; fallbacks `bc5-native`, `bc5-agc` if taken.
- D2 License GPL-2.0 (RPCSX compatibility).
- D3 Public, English, anti-piracy: no Sony material, bring your own console and dumps, no warranty.
- D4 Start with the input layer (`bc5-mount`) before anything GPU-related.
- D5 36/4 CU split as a backend switch, measured in phase 3.
- D6 Tools in Rust, backend in C++20 (RPCSX is C++).
- D7 (2026-09-29) Phase-1 preparation that needs no hardware and no game (Q2 research in the RPCSX sources, `docs/formats/agc.md`, the `bc5-agc` PM4 decoder with hand-built streams) may proceed before gate G0a is recorded, because it does not depend on `bc5-mount`. Phase 0b, anything under `backend/` and anything that submits to the GPU still wait for their gates. Maintainer's decision; overrides the "one phase at a time" rule of `CLAUDE.md` for this case only.
- D10 (2026-09-29) Prosper (github.com/mattias800/prosper, commit `1c93ab8`) is used (*retired as a capture source 2026-09-30 by D11*) **locally on the dev box as an external capture tool** while no firmware dump exists: it runs ASTRO BOT to its title screen without PS5 system modules. Prosper has **no licence**, so none of its code, text or data enters this repository, and nothing derived from it is distributed. It is built from source in the `ubuntu` distrobox (`~/src/prosper`, build in `~/bc5-work/prosper-build`) with one **local-only** patch (`~/bc5-work/prosper-bc5-dcb-dump.local.patch`, 24 lines in `hle_agc.cpp`) that writes each submitted DCB as raw dwords to `$BC5_AGC_DUMP_DIR`; its own `PROSPER_SHADER_DUMP` provides shader binaries. Captures live in `~/bc5-data/captures` and are never committed; experiments record only statistics produced by our tools. Caveat for every result: part of each DCB is written by Prosper's HLE of `sceAgcDcb*` rather than Sony's library, so captures describe "game + Prosper", not a console byte stream. Maintainer's decision.
- D11 (2026-09-30) **The host runtime becomes pluggable; track B is opened.** Following F16–F18: (1) the BC5 backend gets its host interface at the `/dev/gc` boundary (submit ioctls, `mmap` of GPU memory, flexible/tool memory, `kevent`, EOP flip, buffer labels), so any host that provides those calls can drive it. (2) Track A — RPCSX with the maintainer's own firmware dump — stays the reference path and keeps gate G0b. *Track A and gate G0b retired 2026-10-19 by D18 (ADR 0012).* (3) Track B — KytyPlus (GPL-2.0-only, licence-compatible) with the game's own `fakelib/libSceAgc.sprx` and `libSceAgcDriver.sprx` LLE-loaded from the maintainer's dump. First task: implement in KytyPlus the 29 imports of experiment 0010 so that Sony's driver initialises, and route the `/dev/gc` ioctls to a capture buffer; that yields the first DCB built by Sony's code. KytyPlus changes are kept as patches under `backend/kytyplus-patches/` and may be offered upstream as PRs. (4) Phase 1 no longer depends on firmware: its captures come from track B. Prosper (D10) is retired as a capture source. Maintainer's decision, approved in chat ("ok - działaj, zatwierdzam").
- D9 (2026-09-29) Phase-2 preparation (ADR 0004: PM4 builder, memset IB, `dispatch-min` with `--dump-ib`/`--info`) may proceed before gates G0b and G1, because phase 0b is blocked on the maintainer's firmware dump and the preparation submits nothing. Running `dispatch-min --submit` still needs the maintainer's explicit go-ahead with the box idle, each time. *2026-09-30: first go-ahead used; experiment 0008 passed (phase 2 task 2 done), one encoder bug found and fixed on the way.*
- D8 (2026-09-29) BIOS memory split: **512 MiB VRAM carve-out** (amdgpu: VRAM 512M, GTT 7596M; Linux sees 14 GiB) instead of the 8 GiB carve-out recorded in experiment 0003. Rationale: the PS5 memory model is unified and phases 2–3 map guest memory as system RAM (userptr/GTT, Q1); RPCSX needs host RAM for the 16 GB guest address space. Risk: Vulkan paths that insist on a large device-local heap; if VSH boot (phase 0b) fails on memory, compare splits in a dedicated experiment.
- D12 (2026-10-01) **`ttm.pages_limit=3407872` (13 GiB) on the dev box's kernel command line**, set by the maintainer after runs 96–99 (F44–F47): amdgpu now reports 13,312 MiB of GTT instead of 7,597 (`mem_info_gtt_total` = pages limit × 4096 before and after, which settles the `TODO(verify)` of F44 by measurement). Rationale: the game reserves 12.4 GiB of direct memory and a draw's BO list names all of it that holds data. Risk: with 15.2 GiB of RAM, pinned game memory leaves little for the rest of the system; undo with `rpm-ostree kargs --delete=ttm.pages_limit=3407872`.
- D13 (2026-10-06) **After a cold boot the BC-250 comes up with 6 cores / 12 threads; a warm reboot is needed for 8 / 16.** The 8-core unlock service sets an SMU mask that a warm reset keeps and a power cycle loses, and it only re-arms the mask for the next reboot. Boots of 2026-10-02 08:43 and 2026-10-06 16:11 ran on 12 processors (run 117 and the pad tests); the boots of 2026-10-01 had 16. Check with `nproc` before a measurement; the fix is one reboot. Source: `journalctl -b <n> | grep smpboot` and the unlock script's own comment.
- D14 (2026-10-06) **Controller mapping is a text file, not an environment variable per case.** KytyPlus had `--keymap Control=Input` (keyboard, mouse, pad buttons as sources for the host-input stream) but no way to disable a physical pad input and no file. Patch 0001 adds `--input-config <file>` / `KYTY_BC5_INPUT_CONFIG` with the same grammar plus `Pad:<button|axis>=none`, pad axes as sources (`Pad:righttrigger`, `Pad:leftx-`) and `DefaultKeys=on|off`; the built-in keyboard layout stays unless a line takes a key or a control from it. The maintainer's immediate case (a pad whose R2 is stuck: `Pad:righttrigger=none`, `R2=O`) is two lines of `~/bc5-work/input.cfg`, named from `bc5-run.env`. Reference: `backend/kytyplus-patches/input.example.cfg`. 2026-10-08: the same file is edited from a menu in the game window (F2; `settingsOverlay.cpp`, ImGui in the presentation pass beside the IME keyboard, the simplest version first as decided with the maintainer): bind by pressing the input, pad inputs to ignore, the built-in layout, Apply live, Save to the file. The map holds the live configuration behind one mutex and is replaced whole on Apply; a bad line leaves it untouched and is shown. Confirmed by the maintainer in the game on 2026-10-08: F2 opens it, Bind takes the pressed input.
- D15 (2026-10-09) **Per-title settings for the direct path, in the title's launcher, not in the shared `bc5-run.env`.** A title's GPU heaps (`BC5_DIRECT_EAGER_NAMES`, by the names the title gives them) and its learned-fault file (`BC5_DIRECT_LEARNED`) belong to that title: the second title's learned windows had been written into ASTRO BOT's file (51 entries, removed). The second title's launchers are `run-pkg.sh` and `play-pkg.sh` (experiment 0037), the latter behind a desktop shortcut.
- D17 (2026-10-19) **The emulator window has a menu bar; the host still mounts nothing** (ADR 0011, experiment 0164). An ImGui menu bar (File: Open game image / Open package / Recent / Close session / Quit; Emulation: Pause, Restart, Settings, Fullscreen; Help: About) drawn in the settings overlay's pass, `kyty_emulator --menu` for the empty window with a launcher page, F1 or the pad's PS button during play. Opening and closing are exit codes like the F2 menu's 75: 76 = open the path in `$KYTY_BC5_MENU_DIR/choice.txt`, 77 = close the session; `play-any.sh` on the dev box loops over them and hands a `.pkg` to `play-pkg.sh` and a `.ffpfsc` to `play-astro.sh`, so the mount, the system-module pack and the per-title switches stay where D15 put them.
- D18 (2026-10-19) **The host does not use a PS5 firmware or system-software dump, and never will; every system library is written independently in the host** (ADR 0012). After the work of experiments 0154–0164 it is clear the project will not take the RPCSX road (booting the console's system modules): everything both titles needed was written in the host from public symbol names and the titles' own calls, following KytyPlus's model, and that is the rule from here. Track A of D11, phase 0b and phase 4 are retired; RPCSX stays a reference for the AGC/GPU side only (§6). The maintainer's own decrypted system-software root (`~/bc5-data/ps5-fw`) is private reference data: nothing is loaded or derived from it. The system-module pack (`SHADPS4_SYSMODULES_PACK_DIR`), today a copy of a game dump's `fakelib/` — `libSceAgc`, `libSceAgcDriver` (the LLE pair of D11), `libScePlayGo`, `libScePsml` — is to be phased out of the launchers as the host's own libraries cover what it provides, PlayGo and Psml first, the AGC pair last with an independently written driver (Q14). Maintainer's decision, stated 2026-10-19.
- D19 (recorded 2026-10-24; the maintainer's standing rule) **Integration rule for play.** A fix is a diff in its experiment directory, built privately and tested in play or on the bench; it goes into both shared builds only with the maintainer's yes, and `backend/kytyplus-patches/0001-bc5-lle-agc.patch` on main always matches what the shared builds were built from. A speed-up with no regression goes into use even when the root cause is not found (F273, F311; `docs/PHASES.md` phase 5; `PROMPT.md`).
- D16 (2026-10-09) **Conversions that make the GPU execute work it never executed are off by default and tried one short run at a time, with the build synced to disk first.** Run 64 (F90) showed that such a change can take the machine down, and that a hard reset loses what was written just before it (the backend library came back empty). The compute-form `DISPATCH_INDIRECT` conversion stays behind `BC5_DIRECT_MEC_INDIRECT=1`; the second title's launchers turn it on with its one hanging program skipped (F91), after it ran clean in short, watched runs.
